U7-SUM-V3-Q13
Section 7.3 | Summative Assessment | Unit-Synthesis | DOK 2

Unit 7 Summative - Version 3

Solve $y'=4x/y$ by separation of variables. Give an implicit general solution and explain where the constant of integration enters.

Use AP-calculus notation, preserve constants and initial conditions carefully, and include contextual interpretation or units when requested.

Use a complete sentence when the response is an interpretation.

Answer: $y^2=4x^2+C$
Solution / scoring notes: Separate, integrate both sides, and combine the two integration constants into one arbitrary constant.
U7-SUM-V3-Q14
Section 7.4 | Summative Assessment | Unit-Synthesis | DOK 2

Unit 7 Summative - Version 3

The general solution of a differential equation is $y=Ce^{-1x}$. Use the initial condition $y(0)=2$ to find the particular solution and explain how the initial point selects one member of the family.

Use AP-calculus notation, preserve constants and initial conditions carefully, and include contextual interpretation or units when requested.

Answer: $y=2e^{-1x}$
Solution / scoring notes: Substitute the initial condition to determine C, then interpret that fixed C as the selected family member.
U7-SUM-V3-Q15
Section 7.5 | Summative Assessment | Unit-Synthesis | DOK 3

Unit 7 Summative - Version 3

A quantity satisfies a proportional-rate model and has $Q(0)=100$ and $Q(2)=300$. Determine the differential equation and particular model, then interpret the sign and units of the proportionality constant.

Use AP-calculus notation, preserve constants and initial conditions carefully, and include contextual interpretation or units when requested.

Answer: $Q'=(\frac{\log{\left(3 \right)}}{2})Q$ and $Q(t)=100e^{(\frac{\log{\left(3 \right)}}{2})t}$
Solution / scoring notes: Use Q=Q0 e^(kt) to determine k; k has reciprocal-time units and its sign gives growth or decay.
U7-SUM-V3-Q16
Section 7.1 | Summative Assessment | Unit-Synthesis | DOK 2

Unit 7 Summative - Version 3

A population begins at 180 and its rate of change is 7% of the current population per hour. Write a differential equation, propose the corresponding particular exponential solution, and verify the proposed solution by differentiation.

Use AP-calculus notation, preserve constants and initial conditions carefully, and include contextual interpretation or units when requested.

Write only the information needed to resolve the stated question.

Answer: $P'=\frac{7}{100}P$ and $P(t)=180e^{\frac{7}{100}t}$
Solution / scoring notes: Differentiate the proposed exponential and show the derivative equals the model right-hand side.
U7-SUM-V3-Q17
Section 7.2 | Summative Assessment | Unit-Synthesis | DOK 3

Unit 7 Summative - Version 3

The figure shows the slope field for $\frac{dy}{dx}=- x$. (a) State the slope at $(0,2)$. (b) Identify the horizontal-slope locus. (c) Describe how a solution through the point should initially move and justify each conclusion.

Use AP-calculus notation, preserve constants and initial conditions carefully, and include contextual interpretation or units when requested.

Answer: 0; $x=0$; horizontally at first.
Solution / scoring notes: Use the equation for the numerical slope and zero-slope locus, then reconcile those with the field.
U7-SUM-V3-Q18
Section 7.3 | Summative Assessment | Unit-Synthesis | DOK 3

Unit 7 Summative - Version 3

A student solves $y'=y(y-2)$ by dividing by $y(y-2)$, integrating, and presenting only nonconstant solutions. Analyze the work, identify what may have been lost, and state what must be added to make the solution set complete.

Use AP-calculus notation, preserve constants and initial conditions carefully, and include contextual interpretation or units when requested.

Use a complete sentence when the response is an interpretation.

If a value is an estimate, label it as an estimate rather than an equality.

Answer: $y=0$ and $y=2$ must also be checked and included when valid.
Solution / scoring notes: Division by y(y-2) excludes its zeros, which can be equilibrium solutions.
U7-SUM-V3-Q19
Section 7.4 | Summative Assessment | Unit-Synthesis | DOK 2

Unit 7 Summative - Version 3

The general solution of a differential equation is $y=Ce^{2x}$. Use the initial condition $y(1)=3$ to find the particular solution and explain how the initial point selects one member of the family.

Use AP-calculus notation, preserve constants and initial conditions carefully, and include contextual interpretation or units when requested.

Answer: $y=\frac{3}{e^{2}}e^{2x}$
Solution / scoring notes: Substitute the initial condition to determine C, then interpret that fixed C as the selected family member.
U7-SUM-V3-Q20
Section 7.5 | Summative Assessment | Unit-Synthesis | DOK 3

Unit 7 Summative - Version 3

The figure shows an exponential quantity versus time. Classify the behavior, state the sign of $k$ in $Q'=kQ$, and explain why a constant additive-change model would not match the graph.

Use AP-calculus notation, preserve constants and initial conditions carefully, and include contextual interpretation or units when requested.

Use a complete sentence when the response is an interpretation.

Answer: decay; k is negative.
Solution / scoring notes: The curvature/multiplicative change is consistent with an exponential proportional-rate model, not a fixed additive change.
